{"parentUuid":null,"isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"abcc4e3c382b375f0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":"Deeply study the LLM/MODEL/PROVIDER layer, the CLI/interface, and telemetry of Strix (an open-source agentic AI pentest tool) at /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/strix. A hard requirement: we will REPLACE every OpenAI / non-Anthropic model usage with Anthropic Claude models + Claude-native agents. So I need an EXHAUSTIVE map of every point Strix touches a model provider.\n\nRead and report in DEPTH:\n1. **The model/provider wiring — ENUMERATE EVERY REFERENCE.** Strix depends on `openai-agents[litellm]==0.14.6`, `openai>=2.26.0`, `litellm`. Grep the whole tree for: `openai`, `OpenAI`, `litellm`, `gpt-`, `o1`, `o3`, `model=`, `Agent(`, `Runner`, `ModelSettings`, `LitellmModel`, `set_default_openai`, `AsyncOpenAI`, `ANTHROPIC`, `anthropic`, `claude`, `api_key`, `base_url`. For EACH hit: file:line + what it does. Where is the default model name set? Where is the provider/client constructed? Where are API keys read (env vars — OPENAI_API_KEY? ANTHROPIC_API_KEY? LLM_API_KEY?)? Does it already support Anthropic via litellm (e.g. `anthropic/claude-...` model strings)?\n2. **How the OpenAI Agents SDK is used** — strix/agents/factory.py, strix/core/agents.py, strix/core/runner.py. How is the `Agent` / `Runner` from `openai-agents` instantiated? How is the model passed (a LitellmModel wrapper? a model string?)? How are function-tools registered with the SDK? What would it take to point this at Claude — is it just a model-string/provider swap (litellm `anthropic/claude-...`), or is there OpenAI-specific coupling (response format, tool-call schema, structured outputs, reasoning/o1 params, prompt-caching) that would need Claude-specific handling?\n3. **config/settings** — strix/config/{settings.py,models.py,loader.py}: the settings schema for model/provider/keys/temperature/max-tokens/reasoning-effort. Defaults. Env-var names.\n4. **The CLI + TUI interface** — strix/interface/{main.py,cli.py,tui/app.py}: the entry point, the args (target, scope, model selection?), how a run is kicked off, how the TUI displays the agent. Any model-selection flags.\n5. **Telemetry** — strix/telemetry/{posthog.py,scarf.py,logging.py,_common.py}: what does Strix phone home (posthog/scarf are analytics)? This matters — a sovereign/offline fusion must be able to DISABLE all telemetry. How is it toggled? What's sent?\n6. **Prompt-caching / structured output / reasoning** — does Strix use any OpenAI-specific features (JSON mode, structured outputs, o1/o3 reasoning effort, prompt caching) that map differently to Claude (extended thinking, tool_use, prompt caching)? Note them as porting concerns.\n\nBe exhaustive and concrete with file:line + quoted code for the model/provider points especially — this list drives the Claude-replacement workstream. Use it when a task names CRUCIBLE, OBSIDIAN, `python3 -m framework.v2 <subcommand>`, findings/oracles/evidence certificates, the blackboard event spine, critics, reflection, or the reward/calibration loop.\n- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- adversarial-sweep: Independent multi-lens adversarial re-attack of a defensive-oracle domain — the FORGE stage-9 companion to RED-PEN. Attack from disjoint lenses, refute each objection with a real repro, report only what survives. - Before merging any AEGIS/FORGE domain stream, alongside (not instead of) the red-pen agent. Pass the domain spec via args. On the email-auth stream it caught 3 CRITICAL/LOW defects PAST a RED-PEN PASS — near-zero-FP cannot be self-certified by one reviewer.\n- dataviz: Use this skill whenever you are about to create ANY chart, graph, plot, dashboard, or data visualization, in ANY output medium — an HTML or React artifact, inline SVG, plotting code in any library (matplotlib, plotly, d3, Recharts, …), an image/PNG you will render and upload, or a chart shared into Slack. Read it BEFORE writing the first line of chart code, choosing chart colors, building a stat tile / meter / KPI row, or laying out a dashboard. Produces visualizations that read as one system — elegant, accessible, consistent in light and dark — using a brand-neutral placeholder palette you swap for your own. Teaches a design-system-agnostic method: a form heuristic, a color formula with a runnable validator, mark specs, and interaction rules. A validated default palette is documented in `references/palette.md` — swap that file's values for your brand's. Triggers on: \"chart\", \"graph\", \"plot\", \"data viz\", \"visualization\", \"dashboard\", \"analytics\", \"visualize data\", \"categorical colors\", \"sequential / diverging palette\", \"stat tile\", \"sparkline\", \"heatmap\", \"legend\", \"axis\", \"tooltip\", \"chart colors\", \"color by series\".\n- artifact-design: Design guidance and fundamentals for Artifacts.\n- artifact-capabilities: Runtime capabilities a published Artifact page can be granted — behavior static HTML cannot provide on its own, such as the page reading live or connected data, keeping state shared across viewers, or updating and republishing itself. Serves this user's live capability roster and the typed call definitions. Load it whenever the user asks for an artifact needing any such runtime behavior.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by exercising it end-to-end and observing behavior — drive the affected flow, not just tests or typecheck. Run before committing nontrivial changes; bootstraps this repo's project verify skill if none exists yet. Don't invoke it on a diff that only touches tests, docs, or other code with no runtime surface to drive (a change to product source always has one) — there's nothing to observe.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Literal[\"running\", \"waiting\", \"completed\", \"stopped\", \"crashed\", \"failed\"]\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t@dataclass(slots=True)\n27\tclass AgentRuntime:\n28\t    session: Session | None = None\n29\t self.metadata: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n43\t        self.pending_counts: dict[str, int] = {}\n44\t        self.runtimes: dict[str, AgentRuntime] = {}\n45\t        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()\n46\t        self._snapshot_path: Path | None = None\n47\t        self.is_shutting_down = False\n48\t        self._budget_stopped = False\n49\t\n50\t    def set_snapshot_path(self, path: Path) -> None:\n51\t        self._snapshot_path = path\n52\t\n53\t    def mark_shutting_down(self) -> None:\n54\t        self.is_shutting_down = True\n55\t\n56\t    @property\n57\t    def budget_stopped(self) -> bool:\n58\t        return self._budget_stopped\n59\t\n60\t    async def trigger_budget_stop(self) -> None:\n61\t        \"\"\"Signal a scan-wide budget stop and wake every parked agent so it exits.\"\"\"\n62\t        async with self._lock:\n63\t            self._budget_stopped = True\n64\t            for runtime in self.runtimes.values():\n65\t                runtime.wake.set()\n66\t\n67\t    async def register(\n68\t        self,\n69\t        agent_id: str,\n70\t        name: str,\n71\t        parent_id: str | None,\n72\t        *,\n73\t        task: str | None = None,\n74\t        skills: list[str] | None = None,\n75\t    ) -> None:\n76\t        async with self._lock:\n77\t            self.statuses[agent_id] = \"running\"\n78\t            self.parent_of[agent_id] = parent_id\n79\t            self.names[agent_id] = name\n80\t            self.pending_counts.setdefault(agent_id, 0)\n81\t            self.metadata[agent_id] = {\n82\t                \"task\": task or \"\",\n83\t                \"skills\": list(skills or []),\n84\t            }\n85\t            self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime())\n86\t        logger.info(\"agent.register %s (%s) parent=%s\", agent_id, name, parent_id or \"-\")\n87\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n88\t\n89\t    async def attach_runtime(\n90\t        self,\n91\t        agent_id: str,\n92\t        *,\n93\t        session: Session | None = None,\n94\t        task: asyncio.Task[Any] | None = None,\n95\t        interrupt_on_message: bool | None = None,\n96\t    ) -> None:\n97\t        async with self._lock:\n98\t            runtime = self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime())\n99\t            if session is not None:\n100\t                runtime.session = session\n101\t            if task is not None:\n102\t                runtime.task = task\n103\t            if interrupt_on_message is not None:\n104\t                runtime.interrupt_on_message = interrupt_on_message\n105\t\n106\t    async def mark_running(self, agent_id: str) -> None:\n107\t        async with self._lock:\n108\t            if agent_id in self.statuses:\n109\t                self.statuses[agent_id] = \"running\"\n110\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n111\t\n112\t    async def park_waiting(self, agent_id: str) -> None:\n113\t        await self.set_status(agent_id, \"waiting\")\n114\t\n115\t    async def set_status(self, agent_id: str, status: Status | str) -> None:\n116\t        async with self._lock:\n117\t            if agent_id not in self.statuses:\n118\t                return\n119\t            self.statuses[agent_id] = status  # type: ignore[assignment]\n120\t            runtime = self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime())\n121\t            runtime.wake.set()\n122\t        logger.info(\"agent.status %s=%s\", agent_id, status)\n123\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n124\t\n125\t    async def send(self, target_agent_id: str, message: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n126\t        \"\"\"Deliver a user/peer message by appending it to the target SDK session.\"\"\"\n127\t        async with self._lock:\n128\t            if target_agent_id not in self.statuses:\n129\t                logger.debug(\"agent.send dropped unknown target=%s\", target_agent_id)\n130\t                return False\n131\t            runtime = self.runtimes.setdefault(target_agent_id, AgentRuntime())\n132\t            session = runtime.session\n133\t            stream = runtime.stream\n134\t            interrupt = runtime.interrupt_on_message\n135\t        if session is None:\n136\t            logger.warning(\n137\t                \"agent.send dropped target=%s because its SDK session is not attached\",\n138\t                target_agent_id,\n139\t            )\n140\t            return False\n141\t        try:\n142\t            async with session_write_lock(session):\n143\t                await session.add_items([self._message_to_session_item(message)])\n144\t        except Exception:\n145\t            logger.exception(\n146\t                \"agent.send failed to append to SDK session target=%s\",\n147\t                target_agent_id,\n148\t            )\n149\t            return False\n150\t        async with self._lock:\n151\t            self.pending_counts[target_agent_id] = self.pending_counts.get(target_agent_id, 0) + 1\n152\t            self.runtimes.setdefault(target_agent_id, AgentRuntime()).wake.set()\n153\t        if stream is not None and interrupt:\n154\t            stream.cancel(mode=\"immediate\")\n155\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n156\t        return True\n157\t\n158\t    async def wait_for_message(self, agent_id: str) -> None:\n159\t        while True:\n160\t            async with self._lock:\n161\t                if self._budget_stopped or self.pending_counts.get(agent_id, 0) > 0:\n162\t                    return\n163\t                wake = self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime()).wake\n164\t                wake.clear()\n165\t            await wake.wait()\n166\t\n167\t    async def consume_pending(\n168\t        self,\n169\t        agent_id: str,\n170\t        *,\n171\t        include_items: bool = False,\n172\t    ) -> tuple[int, list[Any]]:\n173\t        async with self._lock:\n174\t            count = self.pending_counts.get(agent_id, 0)\n175\t            self.pending_counts[agent_id] = 0\n176\t            session = self.runtimes.get(agent_id, AgentRuntime()).session\n177\t        if count <= 0:\n178\t            return 0, []\n179\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n180\t        if not include_items or session is None:\n181\t            return count, []\n182\t        items = await session.get_items()\n183\t        return count, list(items[-count:])\n184\t\n185\t    async def request_stop(self, agent_id: str) -> None:\n186\t        async with self._lock:\n187\t            if agent_id not in self.statuses:\n188\t                return\n189\t            self.statuses[agent_id] = \"stopped\"\n190\t            runtime = self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime())\n191\t            runtime.wake.set()\n192\t            stream = runtime.stream\n193\t        if stream is not None:\n194\t            stream.cancel(mode=\"after_turn\")\n195\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n196\t\n197\t    async def cancel_descendants(self, agent_id: str) -> None:\n198\t        tasks = []\n199\t        async with self._lock:\n200\t            for aid in reversed(self._subtree_order_locked(agent_id)):\n201\t                task = self.runtimes.get(aid, AgentRuntime()).task\n202\t                if task is not None and not task.done():\n203\t                    tasks.append(task)\n204\t        for task in tasks:\n205\t            task.cancel()\n206\t        if tasks:\n207\t            await asyncio.gather(*tasks, return_exceptions=True)\n208\t\n209\t    async def cancel_descendants_graceful(self, agent_id: str) -> None:\n210\t        async with self._lock:\n211\t            order = self._subtree_order_locked(agent_id)\n212\t        for aid in reversed(order):\n213\t            await self.request_stop(aid)\n214\t        await self._maybe_snapshot()\n215\t\n216\t    async def attach_stream(\n217\t        self,\n218\t        agent_id: str,\n219\t        stream: Any,\n220\t    ) -> None:\n221\t        async with self._lock:\n222\t            self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime()).stream = stream\n223\t\n224\t    async def detach_stream(\n225\t        self,\n226\t        agent_id: str,\n227\t        stream: Any,\n228\t    ) -> None:\n229\t        async with self._lock:\n230\t            runtime = self.runtimes.setdefault(agent_id, AgentRuntime())\n231\t            if runtime.stream is stream:\n232\t                runtime.stream = None\n233\t\n234\t    async def active_agents_except(self, agent_id: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n235\t        async with self._lock:\n236\t            return [\n237\t                {\n238\t                    \"agent_id\": aid,\n239\t                    \"name\": self.names.get(aid, aid),\n240\t                    \"status\": status,\n241\t                    \"parent_id\": self.parent_of.get(aid),\n242\t                }\n243\t                for aid, status in self.statuses.items()\n244\t                if aid != agent_id and status in {\"running\", \"waiting\"}\n245\t            ]\n246\t\n247\t    async def graph_snapshot(\n248\t        self,\n249\t    ) -> tuple[dict[str, str | None], dict[str, Status], dict[str, str]]:\n250\t        async with self._lock:\n251\t            return dict(self.parent_of), dict(self.statuses), dict(self.names)\n252\t\n253\t    def _message_to_session_item(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> TResponseInputItem:\n254\t        sender = str(message.get(\"from\", \"unknown\"))\n255\t        content = str(message.get(\"content\", \"\"))\n256\t        if sender == \"user\":\n257\t            return cast(\"TResponseInputItem\", {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": content})\n258\t        sender_name = self.names.get(sender, sender)\n259\t        msg_type = message.get(\"type\", \"information\")\n260\t        priority = message.get(\"priority\", \"normal\")\n261\t        return cast(\n262\t            \"TResponseInputItem\",\n263\t            {\n264\t                \"role\": \"user\",\n265\t                \"content\": (\n266\t                    f\"[Message from {sender_name} ({sender}) | type={msg_type} \"\n267\t                    f\"| priority={priority}]\\n{content}\"\n268\t                ),\n269\t            },\n270\t        )\n271\t\n272\t    def _subtree_order_locked(self, agent_id: str) -> list[str]:\n273\t        queue = [agent_id]\n274\t        order: list[str] = []\n275\t        while queue:\n276\t            aid = queue.pop()\n277\t            order.append(aid)\n278\t            queue.extend(child for child, parent in self.parent_of.items() if parent == aid)\n279\t        return order\n280\t\n281\t    async def snapshot(self) -> dict[str, Any]:\n282\t        async with self._lock:\n283\t            return {\n284\t                \"statuses\": {"parentUuid":"d0d53063-0711-4644-9e9b-d12013b71da7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"abcc4e3c382b375f0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DwbrbJ4Qey9kKvibRJteaw","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n2\t\n3\timport logging\n4\timport platform\n5\timport sys\n6\tfrom importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version\n7\tfrom pathlib import Path\n8\tfrom typing import Any\n9\tfrom uuid import uuid4\n10\t\n11\t\n12\tlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n13\t\n14\tSESSION_ID: str = uuid4().hex[:16]\n15\t\n16\t_FIRST_RUN_CACHED: bool | None = None\n17\t\n18\t\n19\tdef get_version() -> str:\n20\t    try:\n21\t        return version(\"strix-agent\")\n22\t    except PackageNotFoundError:\n23\t        logger.debug(\"strix-agent version lookup failed\", exc_info=True)\n24\t        return \"unknown\"\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef is_first_run() -> bool:\n28\t    global _FIRST_RUN_CACHED  # noqa: PLW0603\n29\t    if _FIRST_RUN_CACHED is not None:\n30\t        return _FIRST_RUN_CACHED\n31\t    marker = Path.home() / \".strix\" / \".seen\"\n32\t    if marker.exists():\n33\t        _FIRST_RUN_CACHED = False\n34\t        return False\n35\t    try:\n36\t        marker.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n37\t        marker.touch()\n38\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001, S110\n39\t        pass  # nosec B110\n40\t    _FIRST_RUN_CACHED = True\n41\t    return True\n42\t\n43\t\n44\tdef base_props() -> dict[str, Any]:\n45\t    return {\n46\t        \"os\": platform.system().lower(),\n47\t        \"arch\": platform.machine(),\n48\t        \"python\": f\"{sys.version_info.major}.{sys.version_info.minor}\",\n49\t        \"strix_version\": get_version(),\n50\t    }\n51\t"}]},"uuid":"e3ad9f9e-8c84-42d1-8c3f-4d76cb8b3646","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:25:31.737Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d0d53063-0711-4644-9e9b-d12013b71da7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"4a144d59-3e33-4507-9648-c67bc507408c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"3bfd3d93-3d8c-4d5a-8244-149007afff7e","agentId":"abcc4e3c382b375f0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WEuFYRWF8R5nDk7WSNoeX6","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Per-scan logging setup.\"\"\"\n2\t\n3\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n4\t\n5\timport contextlib\n6\timport logging\n7\timport os\n8\timport warnings\n9\tfrom contextvars import ContextVar\n10\tfrom pathlib import Path  # noqa: TC003  used at runtime by ``setup_scan_logging``\n11\tfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\n12\t\n13\t\n14\tif TYPE_CHECKING:\n15\t    from collections.abc import Callable\n16\t\n17\t\n18\t_SCAN_ID: ContextVar[str | None] = ContextVar(\"strix_scan_id\", default=None)\n19\t_AGENT_ID: ContextVar[str | None] = ContextVar(\"strix_agent_id\", default=None)\n20\t\n21\t\n22\tdef set_scan_id(scan_id: str) -> None:\n23\t    \"\"\"Set the scan_id seen on every log record from this point in the task tree.\"\"\"\n24\t    _SCAN_ID.set(scan_id)\n25\t\n26\t\n27\tdef set_agent_id(agent_id: str | None) -> None:\n28\t    \"\"\"Set or clear the agent_id seen on every log record from this point.\n29\t\n30\t    ``None`` clears (renders as ``-`` in the log line). Mutations are\n31\t    isolated to the current asyncio task and tasks created from it after\n32\t    the call.\n33\t    \"\"\"\n34\t    _AGENT_ID.set(agent_id)\n35\t\n36\t\n37\tclass _StrixContextFilter(logging.Filter):\n38\t    def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:\n39\t        record.scan_id = _SCAN_ID.get() or \"-\"\n40\t        record.agent_id = _AGENT_ID.get() or \"-\"\n41\t        return True\n42\t\n43\t\n44\t_FORMAT = \"%(asctime)s.%(msecs)03d %(levelname)-7s %(scan_id)s %(agent_id)s %(name)s: %(message)s\"\n45\t_DATEFMT = \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"\n46\t\n47\t\n48\t# Third-party loggers that get noisy at DEBUG. Capped so the file isn't\n49\t# drowned in their internals when STRIX_DEBUG=1.\n50\t_NOISY_LIBS: tuple[str, ...] = (\n51\t    \"httpx\",\n52\t    \"httpcore\",\n53\t    \"urllib3\",\n54\t    \"litellm\",\n55\t    \"openai\",\n56\t    \"anthropic\",\n57\t)\n58\t\n59\t\n60\t_HANDLER_TAG = \"_strix_scan_handler\"\n61\t\n62\t\n63\t# ``openai.agents`` is the openai-agents SDK's canonical logger root.\n64\t_TRACKED_ROOTS: tuple[str, ...] = (\"strix\", \"openai.agents\")\n65\t\n66\t_STDOUT_QUIET_ROOTS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({\"openai.agents\"})\n67\t\n68\t\n69\tclass _StdoutQuietFilter(logging.Filter):\n70\t    def filter(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> bool:\n71\t        if record.levelno >= logging.WARNING:\n72\t            return True\n73\t        return not any(\n74\t            record.name == root or record.name.startswith(root + \".\")\n75\t            for root in _STDOUT_QUIET_ROOTS\n76\t        )\n77\t\n78\t\n79\tdef configure_dependency_logging() -> None:\n80\t    \"\"\"Quiet dependency logging/warnings that obscure Strix scan logs.\"\"\"\n81\t    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):\n82\t        import litellm\n83\t\n84\t        litellm_logging = litellm._logging\n85\t        litellm_logging._disable_debugging()  # type: ignore[no-untyped-call]\n86\t\n87\t    logging.getLogger(\"asyncio\").setLevel(logging.CRITICAL)\n88\t    logging.getLogger(\"asyncio\").propagate = False\n89\t    warnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\", category=RuntimeWarning, module=\"asyncio\")\n90\t\n91\t\n92\tdef setup_scan_logging(run_dir: Path, *, debug: bool | None = None) -> Callable[[], None]:\n93\t    \"\"\"Attach scan-scoped handlers; return a teardown callable.\n94\t\n95\t    Args:\n96\t        run_dir: Per-scan output directory. ``{run_dir}/strix.log`` is\n97\t            created if missing and opened append-mode (so re-runs of the\n98\t            same scan_id concatenate cleanly).\n99\t        debug: When ``True``, stderr handler runs at DEBUG instead of\n100\t            ERROR. ``None`` (default) reads ``STRIX_DEBUG`` env: ``1`` /\n101\t            ``true`` / ``yes`` / ``on`` enables debug.\n102\t\n103\t    Returns:\n104\t        A no-arg callable that flushes/closes/removes the handlers this\n105\t        call attached. Idempotent — calling twice is a no-op the second\n106\t        time. Safe to call from a ``finally`` block.\n107\t    \"\"\"\n108\t    configure_dependency_logging()\n109\t\n110\t    if debug is None:\n111\t        debug = (os.environ.get(\"STRIX_DEBUG\") or \"\").strip().lower() in {\n112\t            \"1\",\n113\t            \"true\",\n114\t            \"yes\",\n115\t            \"on\",\n116\t        }\n117\t\n118\t    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n119\t    log_path = run_dir / \"strix.log\"\n120\t\n121\t    formatter = logging.Formatter(_FORMAT, datefmt=_DATEFMT)\n122\t    context_filter = _StrixContextFilter()\n123\t\n124\t    file_handler = logging.FileHandler(log_path, mode=\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n125\t    file_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)\n126\t    file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)\n127\t    file_handler.addFilter(context_filter)\n128\t    setattr(file_handler, _HANDLER_TAG, True)\n129\t\n130\t    stream_handler = logging.StreamHandler()\n131\t    stream_handler.setLevel(logging.DEBUG if debug else logging.ERROR)\n132\t    stream_handler.setFormatter(formatter)\n133\t    stream_handler.addFilter(context_filter)\n134\t    stream_handler.addFilter(_StdoutQuietFilter())\n135\t    setattr(stream_handler, _HANDLER_TAG, True)\n136\t\n137\t    tracked_loggers = [logging.getLogger(name) for name in _TRACKED_ROOTS]\n138\t    for tracked in tracked_loggers:\n139\t        tracked.setLevel(logging.DEBUG)\n140\t        tracked.addHandler(file_handler)\n141\t        tracked.addHandler(stream_handler)\n142\t        tracked.propagate = False\n143\t\n144\t    for name in _NOISY_LIBS:\n145\t        logging.getLogger(name).setLevel(logging.WARNING)\n146\t\n147\t    def _teardown() -> None:\n148\t        for tracked in tracked_loggers:\n149\t            for handler in list(tracked.handlers):\n150\t                if getattr(handler, _HANDLER_TAG, False):\n151\t                    tracked.removeHandler(handler)\n152\t                    with contextlib.suppress(Exception):\n153\t                        handler.flush()\n154\t                        handler.close()\n155\t\n156\t    return _teardown\n157\t"}]},"uuid":"ce3c9298-d21a-47ae-8280-762bdcb298fe","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:25:36.635Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4a144d59-3e33-4507-9648-c67bc507408c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"8d379b67-f1b5-4473-a4df-54d04aa9fb08","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"42b9b1f9-689f-46e0-a1c9-3636d14104d9","agentId":"abcc4e3c382b375f0","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018d3bLTC2uvC5a1R68pD91k","type":"tool_result","content":"########## telemetry/__init__ ##########\nfrom . import posthog, scarf\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"posthog\",\n    \"scarf\",\n]\n########## telemetry/README ##########\n### Overview\n\nTo help make Strix better for everyone, we collect anonymized data that helps us understand how to better improve our AI security agent for our users, guide the addition of new features, and fix common errors and bugs. This feedback loop is crucial for improving Strix's capabilities and user experience.\n\nWe use [PostHog](https://posthog.com), an open-source analytics platform, for data collection and analysis, along with [Scarf](https://scarf.sh). Our telemetry implementation is fully transparent - you can review the source code ([posthog.py](https://github.com/usestrix/strix/blob/main/strix/telemetry/posthog.py), [scarf.py](https://github.com/usestrix/strix/blob/main/strix/telemetry/scarf.py)) to see exactly what we track.\n\n### Telemetry Policy\n\nPrivacy is our priority. All collected data is anonymized by default. Each session gets a random UUID that is not persisted or tied to you. Your code, scan targets, vulnerability details, and findings always remain private and are never collected.\n\n### What We Track\n\nWe collect only very **basic** usage data including:\n\n**Session Errors:** Duration and error types (not messages or stack traces)\\\n**System Context:** OS type, architecture, Strix version\\\n**Scan Context:** Scan mode (quick/standard/deep), scan type (whitebox/blackbox)\\\n**Model Usage:** Which LLM model is being used (not prompts or responses)\\\n**Feature Usage:** Which built-in skills are loaded\\\n**Aggregate Metrics:** Vulnerability counts by severity and weakness category (CWE)\n\n### What We **Never** Collect\n\n- Usernames, or any identifying information\n- Scan targets, file paths, target URLs, or domains\n- Vulnerability details, descriptions, or code\n- LLM requests and responses\n\n### How to Opt Out\n\nTelemetry in Strix is entirely **optional**:\n\n```bash\nexport STRIX_TELEMETRY=0\n```\n\nYou can set this environment variable before running Strix to disable **all** telemetry.\n########## pyproject deps ##########\n[project]\nname = \"strix-agent\"\nversion = \"1.1.0\"\ndescription = \"Open-source AI Hackers for your apps\"\nreadme = \"README.md\"\nlicense = \"Apache-2.0\"\nrequires-python = \">=3.12\"\nauthors = [\n  { name = \"Strix\", email = \"hi@usestrix.com\" },\n]\nkeywords = [\n  \"cybersecurity\",\n  \"security\",\n  \"vulnerability\",\n  \"scanner\",\n  \"pentest\",\n  \"agent\",\n  \"ai\",\n  \"cli\",\n]\nclassifiers = [\n  \"Development Status :: 3 - Alpha\",\n  \"Intended Audience :: Information Technology\",\n  \"Intended Audience :: System Administrators\",\n  \"Topic :: Security\",\n  \"License :: OSI Approved :: Apache Software License\",\n  \"Environment :: Console\",\n  \"Programming Language :: Python\",\n  \"Programming Language :: Python :: 3\",\n  \"Programming Language :: Python :: 3 :: Only\",\n  \"Programming Language :: Python :: 3.12\",\n  \"Programming Language :: Python :: 3.13\",\n  \"Programming Language :: Python :: 3.14\",\n]\ndependencies = [\n  \"openai-agents[litellm]==0.14.6\",\n  \"openai>=2.26.0,<2.45\",\n  \"litellm\",\n When responding to search queries:\n\n1. Prioritize cybersecurity-relevant information including:\n   - Vulnerability details (CVEs, CVSS scores, impact)\n   - Security tools, techniques, and methodologies\n   - Exploit information and proof-of-concepts\n   - Security best practices and mitigations\n   - Penetration testing approaches\n   - Web application security findings\n\n2. Provide technical depth appropriate for security professionals\n3. Include specific versions, configurations, and technical details when available\n4. Focus on actionable intelligence for security assessment\n5. Cite reliable security sources (NIST, OWASP, CVE databases, security vendors)\n6. When providing commands or installation instructions, prioritize Kali Linux compatibility\n   and use apt package manager or tools pre-installed in Kali\n7. Be detailed and specific - avoid general answers. Always include concrete code examples,\n   command-line instructions, configuration snippets, or practical implementation steps\n   when applicable\n\nStructure your response to be comprehensive yet concise, emphasizing the most critical\nsecurity implications and details.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _do_search(query: str) -> dict[str, Any]:  # noqa: PLR0911 - each error class needs its own sanitized return\n    if not query or not query.strip():\n        return {\"success\": False, \"error\": \"Query cannot be empty\"}\n\n    api_key = load_settings().integrations.perplexity_api_key\n    if not api_key:\n        logger.warning(\"web_search invoked without PERPLEXITY_API_KEY configured\")\n        return {\n            \"success\": False,\n            \"error\": (\n                \"Web search is not configured for this scan \"\n                \"(operator needs to set PERPLEXITY_API_KEY). Proceed without it\"\n            ),\n        }\n    logger.info(\"web_search query (len=%d): %s\", len(query), query[:120])\n\n    url = \"https://api.perplexity.ai/chat/completions\"\n    headers = {\"Authorization\": f\"Bearer {api_key}\", \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"}\n    payload = {\n        \"model\": \"sonar-reasoning-pro\",\n        \"messages\": [\n            {\"role\": \"system\", \"content\": _SYSTEM_PROMPT},\n            {\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": query},\n        ],\n    }\n\n    try:","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"ed5bc978-3cf5-46c2-84eb-e2776d76ce0d","timestamp":"2026-07-20T01:26:04.337Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"417ae0ea-ee11-42a1-8181-5fa98c9aebb6","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

\"Overview\"\n3\tdescription: \"Configure your AI model for Strix\"\n4\t---\n5\t\n6\tStrix uses [LiteLLM](https://docs.litellm.ai/docs/providers) for model compatibility, supporting 100+ LLM providers.\n7\t\n8\t## Configuration\n9\t\n10\tSet your model and API key:\n11\t\n12\t| Model             | Provider      | Configuration                    |\n13\t| ----------------- | ------------- | -------------------------------- |\n14\t| GPT-5.4           | OpenAI        | `openai/gpt-5.4`                 |\n15\t| Claude Sonnet 4.6 | Anthropic     | `anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6`    |\n16\t| Gemini 3 Pro      | Google Vertex | `vertex_ai/gemini-3-pro-preview` |\n17\t\n18\t```bash\n19\texport STRIX_LLM=\"openai/gpt-5.4\"\n20\texport LLM_API_KEY=\"your-api-key\"\n21\t```\n22\t\n23\t## Local Models\n24\t\n25\tRun models locally with [Ollama](https://ollama.com), [LM Studio](https://lmstudio.ai), or any OpenAI-compatible server:\n26\t\n27\t```bash\n28\texport STRIX_LLM=\"ollama/llama4\"\n29\texport LLM_API_BASE=\"http://localhost:11434\"\n30\t```\n31\t\n32\tSee the [Local Models guide](/llm-providers/local) for setup instructions and recommended models.\n33\t\n34\t## Provider Guides\n35\t\n36\t<CardGroup cols={2}>\n37\t  <Card title=\"OpenAI\" href=\"/llm-providers/openai\">\n38\t    GPT-5.4 models.\n39\t  </Card>\n40\t  <Card title=\"Anthropic\" href=\"/llm-providers/anthropic\">\n41\t    Claude Opus, Sonnet, and Haiku.\n42\t  </Card>\n43\t  <Card title=\"OpenRouter\" href=\"/llm-providers/openrouter\">\n44\t    Access 100+ models through a single API.\n45\t  </Card>\n46\t  <Card title=\"Google Vertex AI\" href=\"/llm-providers/vertex\">\n47\t    Gemini 3 models via Google Cloud.\n48\t  </Card>\n49\t  <Card title=\"AWS Bedrock\" href=\"/llm-providers/bedrock\">\n50\t    Claude and Titan models via AWS.\n51\t  </Card>\n52\t  <Card title=\"Azure OpenAI\" href=\"/llm-providers/azure\">\n53\t    GPT-5.4 via BYOK responses\n557\t    charge only the OpenRouter fee to ``usage.cost`` (often 0) and report the\n558\t    provider charge in ``usage.cost_details.upstream_inference_cost``, so the\n559\t    true BYOK total is the sum of the two.\n560\t    \"\"\"\n561\t    usage: Any = getattr(completion_response, \"usage\", None)\n562\t    if usage is None and isinstance(completion_response, dict):\n563\t usage_cost = _field(usage, \"cost\")\n574\t    if isinstance(usage_cost, int | float) and usage_cost > 0:\n575\t        total += float(usage_cost)\n576\t\n577\t    if bool(_field(usage, \"is_byok\")):\n578\t        upstream = _field(_field(usage, \"cost_details\"), \"upstream_inference_cost\")\n579\t        if isinstance(upstream, int | float) and upstream > 0:\n580\t            total += float(upstream)\n581\t\n582\t    return total if total > 0 else None\n583\t\n584\t\n585\tdef _estimate_response_cost(kwargs: Any, completion_response: Any) -> float | None:\n586\t    \"\"\"Best-effort LiteLLM cost-map estimate when no provider-reported cost exists.\n587\t\n588\t    LiteLLM strips provider cost fields when rebuilding streamed responses and\n589\t    returns no not model.startswith(f\"{provider}/\"):\n614\t        candidates.append(f\"{provider}/{model}\")\n615\t    candidates.append(model)\n616\t    if \"/\" in model:\n617\t No side effects, no new info.\n\n    Use ``think`` when you need a dedicated space to reason before acting —\n    not as an output channel. It's particularly valuable for:\n\n    - **Tool output analysis** — carefully processing the output of a\n      previous tool call before deciding the next step.\n    - **Policy-heavy environments** — when you need to follow detailed\n      guidelines (engagement scope, auth boundaries) and verify compliance\n      before each action.\n    - **Sequential decision making** — when each action builds on previous\n      ones and mistakes are costly (e.g., destructive operations,\n      irreversible auth changes).\n    - **Multi-step exploit planning** — breaking down a complex chain into\n      manageable steps and tracking what's been confirmed vs. assumed.\n\n    Structure your thought to be useful: current state, what you've\n    confirmed, your next planned actions, risk assessment. {"parentUuid":"366384f3-4670-47c1-a3da-6247f90d564c","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"abcc4e3c382b375f0","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdCSWFBE4Ge6X4pm4LuJU","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I now have an exhaustive map. Here is the complete report.\n\n---\n\n# Strix Model/Provider, CLI, and Telemetry — Exhaustive Map for Claude Replacement\n\n## Executive summary (the good news)\n\nStrix does **not** call the OpenAI SDK directly for inference. It uses the **`openai-agents` SDK** (the `agents` package, pinned `openai-agents[litellm]==0.14.6`) plus **LiteLLM** as the universal provider router. Model selection is a **single string** (`STRIX_LLM` env var, e.g. `anthropic/claude-opus-4-8`) resolved through a LiteLLM-backed `MultiProvider` subclass. **Anthropic/Claude is already a first-class, supported path** — it is in the recommended-models list, has a docs page, and routes cleanly through LiteLLM. There is **no hardcoded default model** anywhere; if `STRIX_LLM` is unset the tool errors out. The `openai` PyPI package is imported only for **exception types** (`APIError`, `RateLimitError`) and **response-shape typing** (`ResponseOutputMessage`, `Reasoning`).\n\nBottom line: pointing Strix at Claude is essentially a **config change** (`STRIX_LLM=anthropic/claude-...` + `LLM_API_KEY`/`ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`), not a code port. The code-level work is (a) making Claude the default/validated model, (b) neutralizing OpenAI-specific routing heuristics, and (c) porting the reasoning-effort/tool-schema logic to Claude's extended-thinking semantics. Details and every file:line below.\n\n---\n\n## 1. Model/provider wiring — every reference enumerated\n\n### 1a. Dependencies (`pyproject.toml`)\n- `pyproject.toml:34` `\"openai-agents[litellm]==0.14.6\"` — the agent SDK (imported as `agents`)\n- `pyproject.toml:35` `\"openai>=2.26.0,<2.45\"` — used ONLY for exception classes + response typing\n- `pyproject.toml:36` `\"litellm\"` — provider router\n- `pyproject.toml:57-58` optional extras: `vertex = [\"google-auth>=2.0.0\"]`, `bedrock = [\"boto3>=1.28.0\"]`\n- `pyproject.toml:60` entry point: `strix = \"strix.interface.main:main\"`\n\n### 1b. THE central provider file — `strix/config/models.py`\nThis is the heart of the provider wiring. Every important line:\n\n- **`models.py:8`** `from agents import set_default_openai_api, set_default_openai_key, set_tracing_disabled`\n- **`models.py:9`** `from agents.models.multi_provider import MultiProvider`\n- **`models.py:39-60`** `class StrixProvider(MultiProvider)` — the **provider client**. Overrides `_resolve_prefixed_model`: any prefix that is NOT `openai`/`litellm`/`any-llm` is force-routed through LiteLLM with the prefix preserved (so `anthropic/claude-...`, `deepseek/...` etc. \"just work\"). `ollama/x` → `ollama_chat/x`. This class is instantiated at `runner.py:222`, `dedupe.py:310`, `main.py:337`.\n- **`models.py:63-77`** `DEFAULT_MODEL_RETRY` — SDK-level retry policy (max 5, exp backoff, retries 429/500/502/503/504 + network + statusless provider errors). Provider-agnostic.\n- **`models.py:79-99`** `RECOMMENDED_MODEL_NAMES` tuple — **includes Claude already**:\n  - `models.py:87` `\"anthropic/claude-fable-5\"`\n  - `models.py:88` `\"anthropic/claude-opus-4-8\"`\n  - `models.py:89` `\"anthropic/claude-opus-4-7\"`\n  - `models.py:90` `\"anthropic/claude-sonnet-5\"`\n  - `models.py:91` `\"anthropic/claude-sonnet-4-6\"`\n  - (plus OpenAI gpt-5.x at lines 80-86, Gemini, DeepSeek, Qwen, Kimi)\n- **`models.py:103-113`** `FRONTIER_MODEL_FAMILIES` — quality-gate allowlist; Claude family at `models.py:106-108` (`anthropic`/`bedrock`/`vertex_ai`/... × `claude-fable-5`/`claude-opus-4`/`claude-sonnet-5`/`claude-sonnet-4`).\n- **`models.py:116-131`** `configure_sdk_model_defaults(settings)` — **THE provider bootstrap**, called before every scan (`runner.py:150`, `dedupe.py:308`, `main.py:276`). Does:\n  - `models.py:119` `set_tracing_disabled(True)` (kills SDK OpenAI tracing)\n  - `models.py:120` `_configure_litellm_compatibility()`\n  - `models.py:121` `_configure_openrouter_attribution(llm.model)`\n  - `models.py:122-125` **if `llm.api_key`**: `set_default_openai_key(llm.api_key, use_for_tracing=False)` + set `litellm.api_key` + `_mirror_api_key_to_provider_env(...)`\n  - `models.py:126-131` **if `llm.api_base`**: sets `os.environ[\"OPENAI_BASE_URL\"]`, `litellm.api_base`, and `set_default_openai_api(\"chat_completions\")`; **else** `set_default_openai_api(\"responses\")` ← key OpenAI-specific branch (see §6).\n- **`models.py:134-150`** `_mirror_api_key_to_provider_env` — calls `litellm.validate_environment(model=...)` and copies `LLM_API_KEY` into whatever `*_API_KEY` env var the provider needs (e.g. it will populate `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` from `LLM_API_KEY` automatically). This is how one generic key feeds Anthropic.\n- **`models.py:153-165`** `_configure_litellm_compatibility` — sets `litellm.drop_params=True`, `modify_params=True`, `turn_off_message_logging=True`, `suppress_debug_info=True`, and registers the cost callback. `drop_params=True` is what lets it silently drop OpenAI-only params on Claude.\n- **`models.py:168-188`** `_configure_openrouter_attribution` — injects `HTTP-Referer: https://strix.ai`, `X-Title: Strix`, `X-OpenRouter-Categories: cli-agent` headers (only when model contains `openrouter/`).\n- **`models.py:191-202`** `_register_litellm_cost_callback` — appends `litellm_cost_callback` to `litellm.success_callback` / `_async_success_callback`.\n- **`models.py:205-209`** `_configure_litellm_default(name, value)` — `setattr(litellm, name, value)`.\n- **`models.py:212-219`** `uses_chat_completions_tool_schema(model_name, settings)` — **critical routing heuristic**: returns True (⇒ wrap tools as plain JSON function-tools instead of Responses custom tools) when: model has a non-`openai/` prefix, OR an `api_base` is set, OR the model doesn't support reasoning. **Any Claude model (`anthropic/...`) hits the first condition → always uses the chat-completions tool schema path.**\n- **`models.py:222-233`** `model_supports_reasoning(model_name)` — looks up `litellm.model_cost[name][\"supports_reasoning\"]`. Drives whether reasoning-effort is sent (see §6).\n- **`models.py:236-247`** `is_recommended_or_frontier_model` — used by CLI/TUI warnings.\n- **`models.py:308-315`** `is_known_openai_bare_model(model_name)` — `litellm.model_cost[name][\"litellm_provider\"] == \"openai\"`; used to decide whether a bare (no-slash) name routes to OpenAI.\n\n### 1c. Where API keys / env vars are read — `strix/config/settings.py`\n- **`settings.py:23`** `model: str | None = Field(default=None, alias=\"STRIX_LLM\")` — **no default model; STRIX_LLM is the only model selector**\n- **`settings.py:24-27`** `api_key` ← `AliasChoices(\"LLM_API_KEY\", \"OPENAI_API_KEY\")` — reads **`LLM_API_KEY` or `OPENAI_API_KEY`** (NOT `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` directly — but see `_mirror_api_key_to_provider_env`, which forwards it to `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`).\n- **`settings.py:28-37`** `api_base` ← `LLM_API_BASE`/`OPENAI_API_BASE`/`OPENAI_BASE_URL`/`LITELLM_BASE_URL`/`OLLAMA_API_BASE`\n- **`settings.py:38`** `reasoning_effort` ← `STRIX_REASONING_EFFORT` (default `\"high\"`)\n- **`settings.py:39-42`** `force_required_tool_choice` ← `STRIX_FORCE_REQUIRED_TOOL_CHOICE` (default False)\n- **`settings.py:43`** `timeout` ← `LLM_TIMEOUT` (default 300s)\n- **`settings.py:66`** `TelemetrySettings.enabled` ← `STRIX_TELEMETRY` (default True)\n- **`settings.py:72`** `perplexity_api_key` ← `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` (web_search tool only)\n\n**Answer to \"which env keys\": model=`STRIX_LLM`; key=`LLM_API_KEY` (or `OPENAI_API_KEY`); base=`LLM_API_BASE` (+aliases). There is no direct `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` read — it is auto-populated from `LLM_API_KEY` at `models.py:148-150`.** Note: raw `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` set in the environment would be picked up by LiteLLM/the SDK natively, but Strix's own settings layer does not read it.\n\n### 1d. Other `openai`/`litellm` touchpoints (non-inference)\n- `strix/core/runner.py:14` `from openai import RateLimitError` (caught at `runner.py:381`)\n- `strix/core/execution.py:16` `from openai import APIError` (caught at `execution.py:435`)\n- `strix/core/inputs.py:9` `from openai.types.shared import Reasoning` (used to build reasoning param)\n- `strix/report/dedupe.py:12` `from openai.types.responses import ResponseOutputMessage` — used in `_extract_text` (`dedupe.py:260-269`) to pull text out of a Responses-shaped output. **Porting concern**: LiteLLM/Claude via chat-completions returns a different output-item shape; this `isinstance(item, ResponseOutputMessage)` check may silently return empty for Claude and needs verification.\n- `strix/report/usage.py:140,223` and `state.py:592-628` — LiteLLM cost estimation (`completion_cost`), provider-agnostic.\n- `strix/telemetry/logging.py:54-56,82-85` — silences `litellm`, `openai`, `anthropic` loggers; `logging.py:64,66` tracks `openai.agents` SDK logger root (the SDK's canonical log namespace — cosmetic).\n- `strix/runtime/docker_client.py:19` docstring note: pinned to `openai-agents==0.14.6`; it monkey-patches SDK-internal docker sandbox functions (`_build_docker_volume_mounts`, etc. at `docker_client.py:33-41`) — SDK-version-coupled but provider-agnostic.\n\n**Confirmed: `LitellmModel` is NOT used directly** (grep returned nothing). Routing is entirely via `StrixProvider(MultiProvider)` + model-string prefix. There is **no `AsyncOpenAI(...)`/`OpenAI(...)` client construction** anywhere — only `set_default_openai_key`/`set_default_openai_api` SDK helpers.\n\n---\n\n## 2. How the OpenAI Agents SDK is used\n\n### Agent construction — `strix/agents/factory.py`\n- `factory.py:12` `from agents.sandbox import SandboxAgent` — Strix uses the SDK's **sandboxed agent** variant.\n- `factory.py:454-472` `build_strix_agent(...)` returns `SandboxAgent(name=..., instructions=..., tools=tools, tool_use_behavior=_finish_tool_use_behavior, model=None, capabilities=[Filesystem(...), Shell(...)])`.\n  - **`factory.py:459` `model=None`** — the agent itself carries no model; the model is injected at run time via `RunConfig` (see below). This is why swapping the model is trivial.\n  - `tool_use_behavior=_finish_tool_use_behavior` (`factory.py:307-326`) — custom stop condition: run continues until a lifecycle tool (`finish_scan`/`agent_finish`) reports success.\n- Tools: `_BASE_TOOLS` tuple (`factory.py:329-357`) are all `@function_tool`-decorated functions; extra tools via `register_agent_tools`. **Function-tools are registered by passing the tool objects in the `tools=[...]` list** — standard SDK `FunctionTool`/`CustomTool` objects (`factory.py:15`).\n  - `factory.py:162-167`, `255-267`, `124-159` — when `chat_completions_tools=True` (which is the case for **all Claude models**, see §1b `uses_chat_completions_tool_schema`), SDK `CustomTool`s are **re-wrapped as plain `FunctionTool`s** with a JSON schema (`_custom_tool_as_function_tool`, `_raw_input_schema`), because the Responses \"custom tool\" type isn't available on chat-completions providers. This path is already exercised by Claude and is well-trodden.\n\n### Model injection + Runner — `strix/core/runner.py`\n- `runner.py:12` `from agents import RunConfig`; `runner.py:13` `from agents.sandbox import SandboxRunConfig`\n- `runner.py:149-157`: `configure_sdk_model_defaults(settings)`; `resolved_model = (model or settings.llm.model or \"\").strip()`; raises if empty (`runner.py:152-155`); `chat_completions_tools = uses_chat_completions_tool_schema(resolved_model, settings)`.\n- `runner.py:214-219`: `model_settings = make_model_settings(...)` (see §3/§6).\n- **`runner.py:220-226`** the key wiring:\n  ```\n  run_config = RunConfig(\n      model=resolved_model,                 # model as a STRING\n      model_provider=StrixProvider(),       # LiteLLM-backed provider\n      model_settings=model_settings,\n      sandbox=SandboxRunConfig(client=..., session=...),\n      trace_include_sensitive_data=False,\n  )\n  ```\n  **The model is passed as a plain string + a `model_provider`; NOT a `LitellmModel` wrapper.** The provider resolves the string.\n- `runner.py:227` `hooks = ReportUsageHooks(model=resolved_model, ...)`.\n\n### The actual run loop — `strix/core/execution.py`\n- `execution.py:12` `from agents import RunConfig, Runner`\n- **`execution.py:363-371`** `stream = Runner.run_streamed(agent, input=..., run_config=run_config, context=..., max_turns=..., session=..., hooks=...)` — the single SDK invocation point. Everything is streamed; events are forwarded to `event_sink` (`execution.py:375-378`).\n- `execution.py:411-444` — error handling classifies `MaxTurnsExceeded`/`UserError`/`AgentsException`/`APIError`; strips images and retries on HTTP 400/404/422 input-rejection (`_INPUT_REJECTION_CODES`, `execution.py:42`).\n- `execution.py:387-393` — special-cases a LiteLLM \"after shutdown\" end-of-stream race.\n\n### Direct (non-Runner) model calls — 2 places\n1. **`runner`/`main.py:337-352` `warm_up_llm`**: `model = StrixProvider().get_model(raw_model)`; then `model.get_response(system_instructions=..., input=..., model_settings=ModelSettings(), tools=[], output_schema=None, handoffs=[], tracing=ModelTracing.DISABLED, previous_response_id=None, conversation_id=None, prompt=None)` — a connectivity smoke-test (\"Reply with just 'OK'\").\n2. **`report/dedupe.py:310-326`**: same `StrixProvider().get_model(...).get_response(...)` pattern for the LLM-as-judge dedup call, using `DEDUPE_SYSTEM_PROMPT`.\n\n**What it takes to point at Claude:** Purely a model-string swap (`STRIX_LLM=anthropic/claude-opus-4-8`) — the `StrixProvider`/LiteLLM path already handles Anthropic. The **only OpenAI-specific coupling** that needs Claude-aware handling:\n- `set_default_openai_api(\"responses\")` vs `\"chat_completions\")` branch (`models.py:129-131`) — Claude goes through LiteLLM chat-completions; the \"responses\" default only matters for bare OpenAI. Harmless for Claude but should be reviewed.\n- `dedupe.py:260-269` `ResponseOutputMessage` text extraction — Responses-API-shaped; **verify it extracts text from Claude/LiteLLM responses** (likely needs a fallback).\n- `inputs.py` reasoning param via `openai.types.shared.Reasoning` (see §6).\n- Tool-choice `\"required\"` gating is deliberately restricted to OpenAI models (`inputs.py:27-33`) — Claude never gets `tool_choice=required` today (see §6).\n\n---\n\n## 3. config/settings — schema, defaults, env vars\n\nCovered in §1c. Summary table:\n\n| Setting | Field/type | Env var(s) | Default |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Model | `llm.model: str\\|None` | `STRIX_LLM` | **None (required)** |\n| API key | `llm.api_key` | `LLM_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_API_KEY` | None |\n| API base | `llm.api_base` | `LLM_API_BASE`, `OPENAI_API_BASE`, `OPENAI_BASE_URL`, `LITELLM_BASE_URL`, `OLLAMA_API_BASE` | None |\n| Reasoning effort | `llm.reasoning_effort: Literal[\"none\",\"minimal\",\"low\",\"medium\",\"high\",\"xhigh\"]` (`settings.py:11`) | `STRIX_REASONING_EFFORT` | `\"high\"` |\n| Force tool choice | `llm.force_required_tool_choice: bool` | `STRIX_FORCE_REQUIRED_TOOL_CHOICE` | False |\n| Timeout (s) | `llm.timeout: int` | `LLM_TIMEOUT` | 300 |\n| Telemetry | `telemetry.enabled: bool` | `STRIX_TELEMETRY` | **True** |\n| Sandbox image | `runtime.image` | `STRIX_IMAGE` | `ghcr.io/usestrix/strix-sandbox:1.0.0` |\n| Perplexity | `integrations.perplexity_api_key` | `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` | None |\n\n- **There is no temperature or max-tokens setting** — Strix does not expose them (they'd be defaulted by LiteLLM/provider). Only `reasoning_effort` is exposed for controlling generation.\n- **`loader.py`** (`strix/config/loader.py`): `load_settings()` is memoized (`loader.py:29-45`); precedence **env > `~/.strix/cli-config.json` > defaults** (`loader.py:92-127`). `persist_current()` (`loader.py:56-74`) writes currently-set env vars into `~/.strix/cli-config.json` (mode 0600) — this is called at `main.py:860`, so **on first run your `STRIX_LLM`/`LLM_API_KEY` get persisted to disk**. `apply_config_override(path)` (`loader.py:48-53`) supports `--config`.\n\n---\n\n## 4. CLI + TUI interface\n\n### Entry point — `strix/interface/main.py`\n- `main()` at `main.py:843-949`. Flow: `parse_arguments()` → optional `apply_config_override` (`main.py:851-852`) → `check_docker_installed`/`pull_docker_image` → `validate_environment()` (`main.py:857`) → `warm_up_llm()` (`main.py:858`) → `persist_current()` (`main.py:860`) → clone/collect targets → **telemetry `posthog.start`/`scarf.start`** (`main.py:915-916`) → dispatch to `run_cli(args)` (non-interactive) or `run_tui(args)` (TUI) at `main.py:920-923`.\n- **`validate_environment()` (`main.py:79-202`)**: hard-fails (exit 1) if `STRIX_LLM` unset (`main.py:87-88`). `LLM_API_KEY`/`LLM_API_BASE`/`PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` are \"optional\". Example text shown to user hardcodes `openai/gpt-5.4` (`main.py:120,160`) but also mentions `anthropic/claude-opus-4-7` (`main.py:121`).\n- **`warm_up_llm()` (`main.py:269-352`)**: two guards worth noting for the Claude workstream:\n  - `main.py:280-309` — if model has **no `/` and isn't a known OpenAI bare model** and no `api_base`, it exits with an \"UNKNOWN MODEL NAME\" error telling the user to use `provider/model` form (explicitly suggests `anthropic/claude-opus-4-7`, `main.py:297`).\n  - `main.py:311-335` — \"MODEL QUALITY WARNING\" if model isn't recommended/frontier (Claude models pass this).\n\n### Arguments — `parse_arguments()` (`main.py:402-566`)\nFlags: `--target/-t`, `--target-list`, `--mount`, `--instruction`, `--instruction-file`, `--non-interactive/-n`, `--scan-mode/-m` (quick/standard/deep), `--scope-mode` (auto/diff/full), `--diff-base`, `--config`, `--max-budget-usd`, `--resume`.\n**There is NO `--model` / `--provider` / model-selection flag.** Model is chosen **exclusively via `STRIX_LLM`** (env or config file). If the Claude fusion wants a `--model` CLI flag, it must be added here and threaded into `run_strix_scan(model=...)` (the runner already accepts a `model=` override at `runner.py:111`).\n\n### How a run kicks off\n- **CLI (`strix/interface/cli.py`)**: `run_cli(args)` (`cli.py:38`) builds `scan_config` (`cli.py:85-97`), creates `ReportState`, then `await run_strix_scan(scan_config=..., scan_id=..., image=_resolve_sandbox_image(), interactive=..., max_budget_usd=...)` at `cli.py:180-187`. Image comes from `load_settings().runtime.image` (`cli.py:29-35`).\n- **TUI (`strix/interface/tui/app.py`)**: `run_strix_scan(...)` launched in a background thread/event-loop at `app.py:1447-1468` (`_start_scan_thread`), with `interactive=True` and an `event_sink=self._capture_sdk_event` to stream agent events into the TUI. Model is only read for **display**: `app.py:124-129` reads `load_settings().llm.model` and flags a non-frontier model banner via `is_recommended_or_frontier_model` (`app.py:34`). The TUI has **no model-selection UI**.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Telemetry — what phones home and how to kill it\n\nTwo independent analytics sinks, both gated by the **same** flag `settings.telemetry.enabled` (`STRIX_TELEMETRY`, default **True**):\n\n### PostHog — `strix/telemetry/posthog.py`\n- Hardcoded public key `posthog.py:21` `_POSTHOG_PUBLIC_API_KEY = \"phc_7rO3XRuNT5sgSKAl6HDIrWdSGh1COzxw0vxVIAR6vVZ\"`; host `posthog.py:22` `https://us.i.posthog.com`.\n- `_send()` (`posthog.py:29-52`): **first line `if not _is_enabled(): return False`** (`posthog.py:30-32`, `_is_enabled()` = `load_settings().telemetry.enabled` at `posthog.py:25-26`). POSTs JSON via `urllib.request.urlopen(..., timeout=10)`.\n- Events: `scan_started` (`posthog.py:55-73`) sends **model name**, scan_mode, whitebox/blackbox, interactive, has_instructions, first_run, OS/arch/python/version; `finding_reported` (severity, CWE, is_cve) `posthog.py:76-85`; `skill_loaded` (skill name) `posthog.py:88-95`; `scan_ended` (duration, vuln counts by severity, **LLM token/cost aggregates**) `posthog.py:98-142`; `error` (error_type only) `posthog.py:145-147`.\n\n### Scarf — `strix/telemetry/scarf.py`\n- Endpoint `scarf.py:24` `https://strix.gateway.scarf.sh`. Same enable gate (`scarf.py:27-32`), same event set (`scan_started`/`finding`/`skill_loaded`/`scan_ended`/`error`), sent as **GET/POST URL path + query params** (`scarf.py:31-53`).\n\n### Shared metadata — `strix/telemetry/_common.py`\n- `SESSION_ID` = random `uuid4().hex[:16]`, **not persisted** (`_common.py:14`).\n- `base_props()` (`_common.py:44-50`): OS, arch, python major.minor, strix version.\n- `is_first_run()` (`_common.py:27-41`): touches `~/.strix/.seen` marker.\n\n### What's NEVER sent (per `telemetry/README.md`): usernames, scan targets, file paths, URLs, domains, vulnerability details/code, LLM prompts/responses. Only anonymized aggregates + which model string is used.\n\n### Where events fire\n- `main.py:915-916` start, `928-929` error, `939-940` end\n- `report/state.py:290-291` finding, `350-351` scan-end (finished_by_tool)\n- `skills/__init__.py:188-189` skill_loaded\n\n### How to disable (sovereign/offline requirement)\n**`export STRIX_TELEMETRY=0`** (documented in `telemetry/README.md`) — flips `TelemetrySettings.enabled` False, and every `_send()` in both sinks early-returns. This is the single kill switch and it is clean (guards are the first statement in each send path). For a hardened offline build you could additionally: set the default to `False` at `settings.py:66`, or stub `posthog`/`scarf` — but the env var alone fully disables all outbound analytics. Note the two hardcoded endpoints (posthog.py:22, scarf.py:24) and the Perplexity endpoint (`web_search/tool.py:63` `https://api.perplexity.ai/...`) plus OpenRouter attribution headers (`models.py:169`) are the only other outbound-network constants.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Prompt-caching / structured output / reasoning — porting concerns\n\n### Reasoning effort (OpenAI o-series/GPT-5 semantics)\n- **`strix/core/inputs.py:125-148` `make_model_settings`**: builds `ModelSettings(parallel_tool_calls=False, retry=DEFAULT_MODEL_RETRY, include_usage=True, extra_args={\"timeout\": ...})`. Then, **only if `model_supports_reasoning(model_name)` is True** (`inputs.py:138-142`), it adds `ModelSettings(reasoning=Reasoning(effort=reasoning_effort))` where `Reasoning` is `openai.types.shared.Reasoning` (`inputs.py:9`) and effort ∈ none/minimal/low/medium/high/xhigh.\n  - **Claude porting concern**: The `Reasoning(effort=...)` param is OpenAI's reasoning-effort shape. LiteLLM maps `reasoning_effort` to Anthropic's **extended-thinking budget_tokens** for Claude models that support it, and `model_supports_reasoning` reads LiteLLM's `supports_reasoning` flag from its cost map. So whether Claude gets extended thinking depends on LiteLLM's model metadata for the exact `anthropic/claude-...` string. `litellm.drop_params=True` (`models.py:157`) means unsupported params are dropped rather than erroring. **Action item**: verify `supports_reasoning` is True for the target Claude model in the installed LiteLLM version, and that the effort→budget_tokens mapping is what you want; otherwise Claude runs with thinking disabled.\n\n### Tool-choice = \"required\" (OpenAI-only gate)\n- **`inputs.py:27-33` `_accepts_required_tool_choice`** returns True only for `openai/`-prefixed or known-OpenAI-bare models. **`inputs.py:146-147`**: `tool_choice=\"required\"` is applied only when `force_required_tool_choice` AND the model is OpenAI. **⇒ Claude never receives `tool_choice=required` today.** If the Claude-native agents want forced tool use, this gate must be extended to `anthropic/` (Claude does support `tool_choice`), or the behavior intentionally left off.\n\n### Tool schema routing (Responses custom-tools vs chat-completions JSON function-tools)\n- **`models.py:212-219` `uses_chat_completions_tool_schema`** returns True for any non-`openai/` model ⇒ **all Claude models take the chat-completions tool path**. This triggers `_configure_chat_completions_filesystem_tools`/`_custom_tool_as_function_tool` wrapping in `factory.py:162-167,124-159`, converting SDK `CustomTool`s (apply_patch, etc.) into JSON-schema `FunctionTool`s. This path already works for Claude but is a distinct code path from the OpenAI Responses path — the Claude workstream should validate every custom tool (esp. `apply_patch`, `factory.py:68`) round-trips through the JSON-schema wrapper.\n\n### Responses-API vs chat-completions default\n- **`models.py:129-131`**: `set_default_openai_api(\"chat_completions\")` if `api_base` set, else `\"responses\"`. This only affects the **native OpenAI** provider route; Claude via `StrixProvider`→LiteLLM ignores it. No action strictly required, but worth making explicit for a Claude-only build.\n\n### Structured output / JSON mode\n- **No `response_format`/JSON-mode/`json_schema` structured-output usage anywhere.** All direct model calls pass `output_schema=None` (`dedupe.py:320`, `main.py:344`). The dedup judge relies on prompt-instructed \"respond with ONLY JSON\" + manual parsing (`dedupe.py:302-306,344`), not structured outputs. So there is **no OpenAI structured-output coupling to port** — but the text extraction at `dedupe.py:260-269` is Responses-shaped (`ResponseOutputMessage`) and should get a Claude/LiteLLM-shaped fallback (this is the single most likely silent breakage on Claude).\n\n### Prompt caching\n- **Strix does not explicitly configure prompt caching.** It only **reports** cached tokens for display: `interface/utils.py:298` reads `usage.input_tokens_details.cached_tokens`, shown at `utils.py:305-308`. Cost accounting reads provider-reported cost via the LiteLLM success callback (`report/state.py:505-550`) and falls back to `litellm.completion_cost` (`state.py:585-628`, `usage.py:153-213`). For Claude, prompt caching would need Anthropic `cache_control` breakpoints — **not currently emitted**. This is a pure optimization opportunity (extended thinking + prompt caching on the large static system prompt), not a correctness blocker. The usage/cost ledger is provider-agnostic and will read Claude's `cache_read`/`cache_creation` values as long as LiteLLM normalizes them into `cached_tokens`.\n\n### `parallel_tool_calls=False` (`inputs.py:133`)\n- Set globally.
